# UI labels the handbooks quote

Generated by the handbooks line (E57 C6-C11) from the six handbooks and lang/en.json, lang/pl.json: every bold or
italic span that is a whole lang value of the handbook's language, with the key(s) holding that value. A span
several keys share lists them all (first five). Brought up to date after the text lines land: a key whose value
changed is a handbook line to change. The second table is the bold spans that are no lang value (game names,
emphasis, labels drawn by Foundry or Daggerheart, or a label that drifted).

Quoted labels: 3054 (EN 1478, PL 1576).
